- [Massive Supervised Fine-tuning Experiments Reveal How Data, Layer, and Training Factors Shape LLM Alignment Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14681): Shows how data, layer choice, and training factors shape LLM alignment quality across large supervised fine-tuning experiments.
- [Winter Soldier: Backdooring Language Models at Pre-Training with Indirect Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.14913): Studies indirect data poisoning during pre-training as a way to implant language-model backdoors before downstream safety alignment or deployment.
- [Learning-Time Encoding Shapes Unlearning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15076): Shows that how factual knowledge is encoded during training affects later unlearning, with paraphrased learning changing how reliably facts can be erased.
- [The Safety Reminder: A Soft Prompt to Reactivate Delayed Safety Awareness in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15734): Introduces a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, verifier, reward model, or data-generation route for agent safety.
- [Sysformer: Safeguarding Frozen Large Language Models with Adaptive System Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.15751): Learns adaptive system prompts for frozen LLMs, providing a parameter-efficient guardrail that changes safety conditioning without updating model weights.
- [FOCoOp: Enhancing Out-of-Distribution Robustness in Federated Prompt Learning for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16218): Adds model-side coverage for agent and model safety, focusing on OOD-robust federated prompt learning for VLMs.
- [Black-Box Privacy Attacks on Shared Representations in Multitask Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16460): Shows that multitask shared representations can leak sensitive task information even under black-box access, challenging privacy assumptions in representation sharing.
- [Adaptive RMU for SemEval-2025 Task 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16548): Applies adaptive representation-misdirection unlearning to remove factual knowledge from LLMs, adding a shared-task system perspective on layer-sensitive unlearning for privacy and safety.
- [The Hitchhiker's Guide to Efficient, End-to-End, and Tight DP Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16666): Provides end-to-end auditing procedures for differential privacy, focusing on tighter empirical privacy estimates rather than only formal accounting.
- [ReasonGRM: Enhancing Generative Reward Models through Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16712): Improves generative reward models with large-reasoning-model-generated evaluation rationales and an $R^\star$ metric that scores reasoning-path likelihood.
- [MIST: Jailbreaking Black-box Large Language Models via Iterative Semantic Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16792): Searches black-box jailbreak prompts through iterative semantic tuning, optimizing attack wording without white-box gradients.
- [Fine-Tuning Lowers Safety and Disrupts Evaluation Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17209): Shows benign task fine-tuning can reduce safety and destabilize evaluation behavior, adding a practical fine-tuning drift risk.
- [Efficient and Stealthy Jailbreak Attacks via Adversarial Prompt Distillation from LLMs to SLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17231): Distills adversarial prompt-generation behavior from LLMs into smaller models, making jailbreak search cheaper and harder to detect.
- [SUA: Stealthy Multimodal Large Language Model Unlearning Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17265): Studies stealthy attacks against multimodal LLM unlearning, exposing robustness limits of safety-oriented forgetting.
- [Sleek / Step-by-Step Reasoning Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17279): Uses step-by-step reasoning prompts to recover supposedly erased knowledge, making unlearning robustness a model-side safety failure mode.
- [SAFEx: Analyzing Vulnerabilities of MoE-Based LLMs via Stable Safety-critical Expert Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.17368): Identifies stable safety-critical experts in MoE-based LLMs, making expert-level vulnerability analysis a model-side safety entry.
- [Safe Pruning LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.18931): Preserves safety alignment during LoRA adaptation by pruning unsafe directions with distance-guided robustness signals.
- [Enhancing Security in LLM Applications: A Performance Evaluation of Early Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19109): Evaluates early detection systems for LLM application security, comparing how quickly detectors flag malicious or risky interactions before downstream damage.
- [MSR-Align: Policy-Grounded Multimodal Alignment for Safety-Aware Reasoning in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19257): Uses policy-grounded multimodal alignment for safety-aware reasoning in vision-language models, extending safety alignment beyond text-only refusal behavior.
- [Persona Features Control Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19823): Uses model diffing and sparse-autoencoder features to identify persona directions that predict and control emergent misalignment.
- [Retrieval-Confused Generation is a Good Defender for Privacy Violation Attack of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.19889): Defends privacy-violation attacks by paraphrasing user comments into a disturbed database and retrieving irrelevant substitutes, covertly confusing personal-attribute inference without overt refusal.
- [Q-resafe: Assessing Safety Risks and Quantization-aware Safety Patching for Quantized Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20251): Audits safety degradation across quantization methods and calibration datasets, then applies quantization-aware safety patches for compressed LLMs.
- [Leaner Training, Lower Leakage: Revisiting Memorization in LLM Fine-Tuning with LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20856): Re-examines memorization under LoRA fine-tuning, showing that leakage trends differ from full fine-tuning and pretraining under relaxed similarity metrics.
- [ZKPROV: A Zero-Knowledge Approach to Dataset Provenance for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20915): A zero-knowledge dataset-provenance framework for LLMs. Core idea: attach privacy-preserving proofs that responses come from certified datasets without revealing sensitive training data.
- [Towards Generalized and Stealthy Watermarking for Generative Code Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.20926): Designs watermarks for generative code models that aim to remain detectable while preserving code quality and reducing obvious stylistic artifacts.
- [BiMark: Unbiased Multilayer Watermarking for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.21602): Embeds multilayer LLM text watermarks that balance text quality, model-agnostic detection, and message capacity for practical provenance tracking.
- [CoreMark: Toward Robust and Universal Text Watermarking Technique](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23066): Proposes a robust universal text-watermarking scheme for LLM outputs, targeting provenance detection across model families and decoding settings.
- [Why Settle for Mid: A Probabilistic Viewpoint to Spatial Relationship Alignment in Text-to-image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.23418): Models object spatial relationships in text-to-image generation with probability-of-superiority style objectives to improve compositional alignment.
- [Adaptive Localization of Knowledge Negation for Continual LLM Unlearning](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v267/wuerkaixi25a.html): Localizes knowledge negation for continual LLM unlearning, adding a continual-forgetting mechanism for safety and privacy.
- [Comparing Bad Apples to Good Oranges Aligning Large Language Models via Joint Preference Optimization](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.findings-acl.39): Adds model-side coverage for agent and model safety, focusing on joint preference optimization for LLM alignment.
- [Root Defense Strategies](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-long.97): Improves LLM safety at the decoding level, adding an inference-time alignment and defense route.
- [Uncertainty Propagation on LLM Agent](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-long.302): Studies how uncertainty propagates through LLM-agent workflows, making uncertainty handling visible before action decisions are trusted.
- [BadViM: Backdoor Attack against Vision Mamba](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00577): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [3DM: Distill, Dynamic Drop, and Merge for Debiasing Multi-modal Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.findings-acl.722): Debiases multimodal large language models with distillation, dynamic dropping, and merging.
- [HiddenDetect](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-long.724): Detects jailbreak attacks against MLLMs by monitoring hidden states. Core idea: use internal representations as an early warning signal before unsafe multimodal responses are emitted.
- [AGD: Adversarial Game Defense Against Jailbreak Attacks in Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-long.851): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for agent safety and security evaluation. Core idea: focus on adversarial game defense against jailbreak attacks in large language models as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Exploring LLMs' Ability to Spontaneously and Conditionally Modify Moral Expressions through Text Manipulation](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-long.883): Tests whether LLMs alter moral expressions during text manipulation, separating spontaneous value drift from conditional edits requested by prompts.
- [Multilingual Text-to-Image Generation Magnifies Gender Stereotypes](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-long.966): Measures gender-stereotype amplification in multilingual text-to-image generation, exposing how language choice can change demographic bias in generated visual content.
- [Small Changes, Big Impact: How Manipulating a Few Neurons Can Drastically Alter LLM Aggression](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.acl-long.1144): Contributes a model-side method relevant to agent and model safety.
- [DIESEL: A Lightweight Inference-Time Safety Enhancement for Language Models](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2025.findings-acl.1223): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for agent safety and trustworthy agent operation. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.

Core idea: combine principle-based automated rewards with an OPA-style governance layer so compliance constraints are trainable and auditable.

- [Biased Tales: Cultural and Topic Bias in Generating Children's Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.07908): Creates Biased Tales to measure cultural and gender stereotypes in LLM-generated bedtime stories, including appearance emphasis for girls and heritage framing for non-Western children.
- [So let's replace this phrase with insult... Lessons learned from generation of toxic texts with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.08358): Shows synthetic toxic text from activation-patched Llama and Qwen models underperforms human toxic data for detoxification because generated insults lack lexical diversity.
- [Acquiescence Bias in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.08480): Contributes a model-side method relevant to agent and model safety.
- [Bias in the Loop: How Humans Evaluate AI-Generated Suggestions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.08514): Runs a 2,784-participant human-AI annotation experiment showing correction burden and favorable AI attitudes increase overreliance on flawed AI suggestions.
- [Improving LLM Safety and Helpfulness using SFT and DPO: A Study on OPT-350M](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09055): Adds model-side work on training, architecture, post-training, adaptation, verification, or inference for this capability.
- [Character-Level Perturbations Disrupt LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09112): Introduces a model-side method, architecture, training recipe, or data strategy for safety, alignment, watermarking, unlearning, or attack-defense capability.
- [CTCC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.09703): Fingerprints large language models through cross-turn contextual-correlation backdoors, adding a multi-turn ownership-verification and tamper-resistance line.

- [Towards Inclusive Toxic Content Moderation: Addressing Vulnerabilities to Adversarial Attacks in Toxicity Classifiers Tackling LLM-generated Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12672): Adds adversarial-attack defenses for toxic-content classifiers handling LLM-generated content for the Agent Safety Model section.
- [Forget What's Sensitive, Remember What Matters: Token-Level Differential Privacy in Memory Sculpting for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.12958): Introduces PeCL, combining token-level dynamic differential privacy with memory sculpting so continual learners forget sensitive tokens while retaining task-invariant knowledge.
- [CogniAlign: Survivability-Grounded Multi-Agent Moral Reasoning for Safe and Transparent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.13356): Evaluates Survivability-Grounded Multi-Agent Moral Reasoning for Safe and Transparent AI as a safety-relevant attack surface, training signal, evaluation protocol, or risk channel.
- [Perception-Integrated Safety Critical Control via Analytic Collision Cone Barrier Functions on 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14421): Builds or evaluates a guardrail or content-safety method through Analytic Collision Cone Barrier Functions on 3D Gaussian Splatting, focusing on unsafe-output detection and safety-utility trade-offs.
- [Enhancing Retrieval Augmentation via Adversarial Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14750): Contributes a model-side method relevant to agent and model safety.
- [Designing Latent Safety Filters using Pre-Trained Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.14758): Builds or evaluates a guardrail or content-safety method through Pre-Trained Vision Models, focusing on unsafe-output detection and safety-utility trade-offs.
- [Robust Vision-Language Models via Tensor Decomposition: A Defense Against Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16163): Applies plug-and-play Tensor Train decomposition to reconstruct VLM vision-encoder representations, filtering adversarial noise without retraining and recovering CLIP retrieval accuracy on COCO and Flickr30K.
- [DecipherGuard: Understanding and Deciphering Jailbreak Prompts for a Safer Deployment of Intelligent Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16870): Introduces model-side work on decipherGuard: Understanding and Deciphering Jailbreak Prompts for a Safer Deployment of Intelligent Software Systems, fitting training, architecture, post-training, synthetic-data, verifier, reward, or world-model coverage.
- [Safe-SAIL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.18127): Builds a sparse-autoencoder interpretation framework for safety-critical LLM features, reducing explanation cost and mapping fine-grained safety concepts in model activations.
- [Semantic Representation Attack against Aligned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19360): Replaces exact-string jailbreak objectives with semantic representation targets, then uses heuristic search to produce concise adversarial prompts that preserve harmful meaning while improving naturalness.
- [Identifying and Addressing User-level Security Concerns in Smart Homes Using “Smaller” LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19485): Introduces a model-side method, training signal, architecture, or adaptation strategy for Agent Safety.

- [bi-GRPO: Bidirectional Optimization for Jailbreak Backdoor Injection on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19775): Studies jailbreak backdoor injection through bidirectional GRPO, adding a model-side safety-risk line for RL-tuned language models.
- [Voice Privacy Preservation with Multiple Random Orthogonal Secret Keys: Attack Resistance Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.19906): Analyzes an adversarial risk through Attack Resistance Analysis, focusing on the attack surface, transfer path, or defense failure mode.
- [Beyond Sharp Minima: Robust LLM Unlearning via Feedback-Guided Multi-Point Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20230): Improves LLM unlearning with feedback-guided multi-point optimization, adding a robustness-oriented forgetting method.
- [Poisoning Prompt-Guided Sampling in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20851): Introduces PoisonVID, a black-box attack that optimizes universal perturbations to suppress harmful-frame relevance scores in prompt-guided VideoLLM sampling.
- [RLCracker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.20924): Uses adaptive reinforcement-learning attacks to expose LLM watermark vulnerabilities.
- [SafeSteer: Adaptive Subspace Steering for Efficient Jailbreak Defense in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.21400): Uses SVD to build a low-dimensional safety subspace for one-pass inference-time VLM steering, reducing jailbreak signals without weight updates.
- [Bidirectional Intention Inference Enhances LLMs' Defense Against Multi-Turn Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22732): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for agent safety capability coverage.

- [Safety Routing Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22745): Defends MoE LLMs against harmful fine-tuning by aligning safety behavior through routing mechanisms rather than only post-hoc filters.
- [What If Moderation Didn’t Mean Suppression? A Case for Personalized Content Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.22861): Argues for personalized content transformation as an alternative moderation strategy.
- [GuardNet: Graph-Attention Filtering for Jailbreak Defense in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23037): Uses graph-attention filtering for jailbreak defense.
- [Risk Profiling and Modulation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23058): Elicits and modulates LLM risk preferences, comparing prompting, in-context learning, and post-training for behavioral alignment.
- [StolenLoRA: Exploring LoRA Extraction Attacks via Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23594): Adds a LoRA extraction attack using synthetic data for the Agent Safety Model section.
- [Distillation-Conditional Backdoor Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23871): studies backdoors that are activated by model distillation conditions, exposing a safety risk in compression and transfer pipelines.
- [Stable Forgetting: Bounded Parameter-Efficient Unlearning in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24166): Stabilizes LoRA-based foundation-model unlearning with bounded MLP adapter functions, preventing gradient-ascent weight blow-up during forget-retain optimization.
- [AdvChain: Adversarial Chain-of-Thought Tuning for Robust Safety Alignment of Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24269): Uses adversarial chain-of-thought tuning to improve robust safety alignment of large reasoning models.
- [GSPR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24418): Trains LLM safeguards as generalizable policy reasoners so refusal and safety judgments transfer across heterogeneous safety taxonomies.
- [TokenSwap: Backdoor Attack on the Compositional Understanding of Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24566): Attacks LVLM compositional understanding by swapping object-relation tokens rather than forcing a fixed target string, adding a stealthier multimodal backdoor threat model.
- [Inducing Dyslexia in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.24597): Adds model-side coverage for agent and model safety, focusing on dyslexia-style robustness probe for VLMs.
- [VISOR++ Visual Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25533): controls large vision-language model behavior through universal visual inputs, adding a safety-relevant steering method for closed-source VLMs.
- [ASGuard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.25843): Uses activation-scaling interventions on jailbreak-relevant attention heads to mitigate targeted safety bypasses such as tense-changing attacks.
- [SafeBehavior: Simulating Human-Like Multistage Reasoning to Mitigate Jailbreak Attacks in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.26345): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for agent safety capability coverage.

- [GemDetox at TextDetox CLEF 2025: Enhancing a Massively Multilingual Model for Text Detoxification on Low-resource Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01250): Contributes a agent safety model-side method, training recipe, architecture, verifier, reward, world-model, or synthetic-data technique.
- [TRACE / Is It Thinking or Cheating?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01367): Detects implicit reward hacking by measuring whether truncated reasoning already earns high reward, giving CoT monitoring a model-side effort signal rather than relying only on surface text.

- [Machine Learning for Detection and Analysis of Novel LLM Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.01644): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for agent safety and security. Core idea: Large Language Models (LLMs) suffer from a range of vulnerabilities that allow malicious users to solicit undesirable responses through manipulation of the input text.
- [Modeling the Attack: Detecting AI-Generated Text by Quantifying Adversarial Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.02319): Introduces PIFE, normalizing text and feeding perturbation magnitude features such as edit distance and semantic similarity into AI-generated text detectors for paraphrase robustness.

- [From Delegates to Trustees: How Optimizing for Long-Term Interests Shapes Bias and Alignment in LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12689): Compares delegate-style preference cloning with trustee-style long-term-interest optimization, revealing a welfare-autonomy trade-off and model-default bias risk.
- [Content Anonymization for Privacy in Long-form Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.12780): Contributes a model-side method relevant to agent and model safety.

- [Gaussian Certified Unlearning in High Dimensions: A Hypothesis Testing Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13094): Defines epsilon-Gaussian certifiability and shows one Newton unlearning step plus calibrated Gaussian noise can preserve privacy and accuracy in high dimensions.

- [Stealthy Dual-Trigger Backdoors: Attacking Prompt Tuning in LM-Empowered Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.14470): Attacks LM-powered graph foundation models during prompt tuning with coordinated text-level and structure-level triggers that preserve clean accuracy.

- [The Hidden Cost of Modeling P(X): Vulnerability to Membership Inference Attacks in Generative Text Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16122): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [Bridging Symmetry and Robustness: On the Role of Equivariance in Enhancing Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.16171): Embeds rotation- and scale-equivariant convolutional layers into CNNs, improving adversarial robustness and certified bounds without adversarial training.

- [HarmNet: A Framework for Adaptive Multi-Turn Jailbreak Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.18728): Introduces HarmNet, combining a hierarchical semantic network, feedback simulator, and real-time traverser for adaptive multi-turn jailbreak attacks.
- [Subliminal Corruption: Mechanisms, Thresholds, and Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.19152): Quantifies subliminal corruption in GPT-2 teacher-student fine-tuning, showing semantically neutral synthetic data can transmit misalignment after sharp poison thresholds.

- [Whose Narrative is it Anyway? A KV Cache Manipulation Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.12752): Introduces History Swapping, overwriting contiguous KV-cache blocks with precomputed topic caches to steer generation without changing the visible prompt.

- [AI Chatbot Security Enhancement with Adversarial Attack Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icimia67127.2025.11200846): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [Detection of Data Exposures in Software Services Using a Large Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/sse67621.2025.00020): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [A Robust Deep-Fake Video Detection Model Based on Behavioral and Facial Features Using Random Forest Classifier](https://doi.org/10.1109/acit68900.2025.11510578): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [Hybrid Deep Learning Approach For Deepfake Video Detection Using Feature Fusion](https://doi.org/10.1109/icerect65215.2025.11377400): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [An Innovative Model of Deepfake Detection in Video Using 3D EfficientnetB7 with Spatial Attention](https://doi.org/10.23919/indiacom66777.2025.11115212): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [Utilizing rPPG Signal Synchronization and Deep Learning Techniques for Deepfake Video Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2025.3604336): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [A Hybrid Deep Learning Model for Deepfake Video Detection](https://doi.org/10.1109/icacrs67045.2025.11324229): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [Diff-Cleanse: Identifying and Mitigating Backdoor Attacks in Diffusion Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icme59968.2025.11210014): Adds a method for identifying and mitigating backdoor attacks in diffusion models.
- [I know what you MEME! Understanding and Detecting Harmful Memes with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.14722/ndss.2025.240415): Adds multimodal large-language-model detection of harmful memes.
- [CABIN: Debiasing Vision-Language Models Using Backdoor Adjustments](https://doi.org/10.24963/ijcai.2025/55): Adds backdoor-adjustment debiasing for vision-language models.
- [Selective Privacy-Preserving Federated Learning for Large Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://doi.org/10.1109/iwcmc65282.2025.11059634): Adds model-side coverage for agent and model safety, focusing on privacy-preserving federated LLM fine-tuning.
- [Making Theft Useless: Adulteration-Based Protection of Proprietary Knowledge Graphs in GraphRAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00274): Protects proprietary GraphRAG knowledge graphs with AURA, injecting plausible false adulterants so stolen graphs become unreliable in an attacker's isolated environment.
- [FLAGuard: Efficient Verifiable Federated LoRA of Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tmc.2025.3641570): Verifies federated LoRA aggregation in two stages, checking the aggregated low-rank matrices and their multiplication result while compressing gradient matrices with Iterative Gradient Sampling and Convolutional Compression.
- [Stealthier Backdoor Attacks for Multimodal Models: Bidirectional Triggers via Coverless Steganography and Semantic Preservation](https://doi.org/10.1109/miccis66057.2025.00025): Embeds a trigger in one modality and activates the attack in another, using coverless steganography plus semantic-preserving and language-modeling losses to make multimodal backdoors harder to detect and more robust to transformations.
- [EXDF: Explainable Deepfake Detection with Vision-Language Model](https://doi.org/10.1109/icip55913.2025.11084529): Frames deepfake detection as VQA, trains a VLM on the ExDF dataset with GPT-4o mask-guided explanations, and adds a facial-mask encoder so detections identify manipulated facial regions instead of only real/fake labels.
- [Data Poisoning Attacks of Fine-Tuning for Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/aicsip65423.2025.11427210): Proposes JailPoisoning, combining GCG jailbreak generation with fine-tuning data poisoning to create poisoned datasets that bypass safety checks on models such as Qwen2.5 and DeepSeek-R1-Distill.
- [Detecting and Mitigating Social Bias in Transformer Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/gcwcn66157.2025.11448494): Studies detection and mitigation of social bias in transformer language models, adding a model-side safety and fairness signal.
- [Quantifying and Mitigating Occupational Bias in Open-Source Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/bigdata66926.2025.11402061): Quantifies and mitigates occupational bias in open-source large language models.
- [AdvAttack-LLM: Exploiting Elasticity in Large Language Models for Efficient Adversarial Attacks](https://doi.org/10.1109/iccbdai66607.2025.11389048): Studies adversarial attacks that exploit elasticity in large language models.
- [Defensive M2S: Training Guardrail Models on Compressed Multi-turn Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00454): Builds or evaluates a guardrail or content-safety method through Defensive M2S, focusing on unsafe-output detection and safety-utility trade-offs.
- [Post-Processing Watermarking vs Direct Integration into Generative Models](https://doi.org/10.15625/vap.2025.0339): Compares post-processing watermarking with direct watermark integration into generative models.
- [Enhancing Synthetic Generated-Images Detection through Post-Hoc Calibration](https://doi.org/10.1109/wacvw65960.2025.00087): Calibrates detectors for synthetic generated-image detection.
- [TWT-LLM: A Universal and Robust Tagged Watermark for Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/smc58881.2025.11342733): Introduces a universal tagged watermark for large language models.
- [Analysis of the impact of prompt obfuscation on the effectiveness of language models in detecting prompt injections](https://doi.org/10.7256/2454-0714.2025.2.73939): Analyzes how prompt obfuscation affects language models that detect prompt injections.
- [Black-Box Steganography for Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/tcsvt.2025.3574808): Adds a black-box LLM steganography method relevant to covert-channel safety risks.
- [Privacy-Preserving NLP: Federated Learning for On-Device Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://doi.org/10.1109/icsit65336.2025.11294286): Applies federated learning to on-device language-model fine-tuning, adding a privacy-preserving adaptation route.
- [CoRLHF: Reinforcement learning from human feedback with cooperative policy-reward optimization for LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2025.130113): Iteratively co-optimizes the policy and reward model, collecting reward-model preference data from consensus scores on policy rollouts so RLHF can reduce policy-reward distribution mismatch and bridge toward AI-feedback training.
- [Decomposition, Synthesis, and Attack: A Multi-Instruction Fusion Method for Jailbreaking LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1109/jiot.2025.3525741): Studies a multi-instruction fusion method for jailbreaking LLMs, adding an attack-side safety reference.
- [BAIT: Large Language Model Backdoor Scanning by Inverting Attack Target](https://doi.org/10.1109/sp61157.2025.00103): Scans LLM backdoors by inverting attack targets, fitting model-side safety and security diagnostics.
- [Privacy-Preserving Prompt Injection Detection for Smart Cloud-Deployed Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/smartcloud66068.2025.00009): A model, training, architecture, or algorithm entry for agent safety and trustworthy agent operation. Its value is to track capability-relevant model-side progress under the round15 Model-screening rule.
- [Multilevel Distribution Alignment for Multisource Universal Domain Adaptation](https://doi.org/10.1109/tnnls.2025.3561401): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, inference strategy, or algorithmic capability for agent safety.
- [Trajectory Guard - A Lightweight, Sequence-Aware Model for Real-Time Anomaly Detection in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00516): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for agent safety and security evaluation. Core idea: focus on a lightweight, sequence-aware model for real-time anomaly detection in agentic ai as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [Low Rank Comes with Low Security: Gradient Assembly Poisoning Attacks against Distributed LoRA-based LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.00566): Introduces or studies a backdoor or poisoning attack through Gradient Assembly Poisoning Attacks against Distributed LoRA-based LLM Systems, focusing on trigger persistence, transfer, or data-recovery leakage.
- [Reliable Grid Forecasting: State Space Models for Safety-Critical Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01410): Builds or evaluates a guardrail or content-safety method through State Space Models for Safety-Critical Energy Systems, focusing on unsafe-output detection and safety-utility trade-offs.
- [SEAttack: A self-evolving jailbreak attack to induce toxic responses for non-toxic queries in large language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ipm.2025.104544): Uses self-evolving jailbreak attacks to induce toxic responses from non-toxic queries, adding an adaptive attack model for safety evaluation.
- [Structural Representations for Cross-Attack Generalization in AI Agent Threat Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01723): Models structural representations for cross-attack generalization in AI-agent threat detection.
- [UnPII: Unlearning Personally Identifiable Information with Quantifiable Exposure Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01786): Prioritizes LLM unlearning by a PII risk index, forgetting high-exposure personal attributes more aggressively while tracking utility and regulatory deletion needs.
- [MORE: Multi-Objective Adversarial Attacks on Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.01852): Contributes a model-side method relevant to agent and model safety.
- [Break Me If You Can: Self-Jailbreaking of Aligned LLMs via Lexical Insertion Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02670): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
- [Window-based Membership Inference Attacks Against Fine-tuned Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02751): Replaces global-loss membership inference with sliding-window votes, capturing localized memorization signals in fine-tuned LLM text sequences.
- [Quality Degradation Attack in Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.02947): Introduces a model-side method, training signal, architecture, or adaptation strategy for Agent Safety.
- [JPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03005): Bridges jailbreak defense and unlearning through on-policy path rectification, connecting safety alignment with targeted forgetting.
- [Jailbreak-Zero: A Path to Pareto Optimal Red Teaming for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03265): Evaluates red-teaming with A Path to Pareto Optimal Red Teaming for Large Language Models, focusing on diverse harm discovery and guardrail failure modes.
- [TRYLOCK: Defense-in-Depth Against LLM Jailbreaks via Layered Preference and Representation Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03300): Combines DPO safety alignment, representation-engineering steering, a sidecar steering classifier, and input canonicalization into a layered jailbreak defense.
- [ALERT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03600): Detects LLM jailbreaks in zero-shot settings by amplifying internal discrepancy signals.
- [Merging Triggers, Breaking Backdoors: Defensive Poisoning for Instruction-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04448): Introduces MB-Defense, which first merges attacker and defensive triggers into a unified backdoor representation and then neutralizes that representation through additional training while preserving instruction-following behavior.
- [FeedEval: Pedagogically Aligned Evaluation of LLM-Generated Essay Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04574): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, reward, over-refusal, or monitoring method for language or multimodal models.
- [Constitutional Classifiers++: Efficient Production-Grade Defenses against Universal Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.04603): Improves production-grade jailbreak defenses with Constitutional Classifiers.
- [Observations and Remedies for Large Language Model Bias in Self-Consuming Performative Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05184): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
- [Same Claim, Different Judgment: Benchmarking Scenario-Induced Bias in Multilingual Financial Misinformation Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05403): Measures or mitigates fairness and bias risk through Benchmarking Scenario-Induced Bias in Multilingual Financial Misinformation Detection, treating demographic, severity, or moderation skew as the safety axis.
- [Jailbreaking Large Language Models through Iterative Tool-Disguised Attacks via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05466): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, or data method for agent safety capability coverage.
- [Toward an Integrated Cross-Urban Accident Prevention System: A Multi-Task Spatial-Temporal Learning Framework for Urban Safety Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05521): Builds a multi-task spatial-temporal learning model for cross-urban accident prevention and urban safety management.
- [Automated Generation of Accurate Privacy Captions From Android Source Code Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06276): Generates privacy captions directly from Android source code with PCapGen, using code context to describe what personal data an app uses, how, and why.
- [Why LoRA Fails to Forget: Regularized Low-Rank Adaptation Against Backdoors in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06305): Shows LoRA fails to remove LLM backdoors because its updates are spectrally weak and poorly aligned, then introduces RoRA to strengthen clean-task directions and suppress trigger subspaces.
- [Evaluating Cross-Lingual Unlearning in Multilingual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06675): Translates TOFU into seven language and script variants, showing many unlearning algorithms fail outside the training language while subspace projection exploits a shared interlingua geometry for cross-lingual forgetting.
- [A Robust Certified Machine Unlearning Method Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06967): Handles non-i.i.d. unlearning requests with distribution-aware iterative Newton updates constrained by a trust region, tightening certified residual bounds under deletion-induced shift.
- [Safe-FedLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.07177): Studies safety in federated large language models, adding a distributed-training and deployment angle to model-side safety analysis.
- [Detecting Mental Manipulation in Speech via Synthetic Multi-Speaker Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08342): Introduces SPEECHMENTALMANIP, a voice-consistent synthetic multi-speaker audio benchmark, and shows audio-language models have high specificity but low recall for manipulative speech compared with text.
- [Semantic Misalignment in Vision-Language Models under Perceptual Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.08355): Perturbs Cityscapes semantic segmentation inputs and measures hallucinated objects, safety-critical omissions, and inconsistent VLM safety judgments that are not predicted by pixel-level segmentation metrics.
- [A Marketplace for AI-Generated Adult Content and Deepfakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09117): Parent review candidate: analyzes 14 months of Civitai bounty requests, finding NSFW and explicit deepfake commissions concentrated among frequent requesters and disproportionately targeting female celebrities rather than contributing a model method.
- [Reward Learning through Ranking Mean Squared Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09236): Introduces R4, a rating-based RL objective that fits trajectory-rating pairs with ranking mean squared error and gives minimal-complete solution guarantees for ordinal human feedback.
- [SpatialJB: How Text Distribution Art Becomes the "Jailbreak Key" for LLM Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09321): Defends against jailbreak or prompt-injection attacks with SpatialJB, focusing on the bypass channel and refusal or filtering behavior.
- [Bias Dynamics in BabyLMs: Towards a Compute-Efficient Sandbox for Democratising Pre-Training Debiasing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.09421): Uses compact BabyLM BERT-like models as a low-cost pretraining-debiasing sandbox, matching BERT bias dynamics while reducing experiments from more than 500 GPU-hours to under 30.
- [ReasAlign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10173): Introduces Reasoning Enhanced Safety Alignment against Prompt Injection Attack, a model-side contribution for Agent Safety.
- [Predicting Biased Human Decision-Making with Large Language Models in Conversational Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.11049): Runs a preregistered chatbot study with 1,648 participants and tests whether LLMs reproduce framing and status-quo bias shifts under higher dialogue complexity.
- [Preserving Fairness and Safety in Quantized LLMs Through Critical Weight Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12033): Measures or mitigates fairness and bias risk through Critical Weight Protection, treating demographic, severity, or moderation skew as the safety axis.
- [Less Is More - Until It Breaks: Security Pitfalls of Vision Token Compression in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12042): A model, training, architecture, reinforcement-learning, synthetic-data, or inference method for agent safety and security. Core idea: Visual token compression is widely adopted to improve the inference efficiency of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), enabling their deployment in latency-sensitive and resource-constrained scenarios.
- [TrojanPraise: Jailbreak LLMs via Benign Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12460): Fine-tunes models on moderation-approved praise data so a crafted benign word shifts attitude toward harmful concepts, reaching up to 95.88% attack success under black-box fine-tuning.
- [ACE-Align: Attribute Causal Effect Alignment for Cultural Values under Varying Persona Granularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12962): Aligns demographic attribute causal effects on cultural values across 14 countries and four persona attributes, reducing the high-resource versus low-resource alignment gap from 9.81 to 4.92 points.
- [When Reasoning Leaks Membership: Membership Inference Attack on Black-box Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.13607): Proposes BlackSpectrum, which embeds exposed reasoning traces along a recall-inference axis to infer whether prompts came from LRM training data, with arXivReasoning and BookReasoning datasets.
- [BACH-V: Bridging Abstract and Concrete Human-Values in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14007): Probes and steers value representations across abstract descriptions, concrete events, and decisions, showing cross-level transfer and causal effects on value-laden judgments.
- [LLM Security and Safety: Insights from Homotopy-Inspired Prompt Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14528): Systematically obfuscates prompts to study latent LLM safety and security weaknesses across large prompt suites.
- [NeuroFilter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14660): Builds privacy guardrails from linear activation-space directions and activation velocity, detecting contextual-integrity violations inside conversational LLM agents.
- [Obscuring Data Contamination Through Translation: Evidence from Arabic Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.14994): Contributes a model-side method relevant to agent and model safety.
- [Training-Free and Interpretable Hateful Video Detection via Multi-stage Adversarial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15115): Builds MARS, a training-free hateful-video detector that stages neutral video description, supporting evidence, counter-evidence, and synthesized decisions for interpretable moderation.
- [RECAP: A Resource-Efficient Method for Adversarial Prompting in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15331): Retrieves semantically similar successful adversarial prompts from a category-indexed database, reducing the compute cost of GCG/PEZ/GBDA-style red-teaming on Llama 3 8B.
- [YuFeng-XGuard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15588): A reasoning-centric, interpretable guardrail model for flexible fine-grained LLM risk assessment.
- [Connect the Dots: Knowledge Graph-Guided Crawler Attack on Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15678): Connect the Dots adds a safety model for jailbreaks, privacy, guardrails, unlearning, or adversarial robustness.
- [Beyond Visual Safety: Jailbreaking Multimodal Large Language Models for Harmful Image Generation via Semantic-Agnostic Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15698): Uses neutralized visual splicing and inductive recomposition in a reconstruction-then-generation attack to make MLLMs generate harmful images from semantic-agnostic inputs.
- [Safety Vectors via Global Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.15801): Attributes and optimizes safety vectors in LLMs. Core idea: identify representation directions that mediate safety behavior and use global optimization to control them.
- [Unintended Memorization of Sensitive Information in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17480): Probes PII that appears only in fine-tuning inputs, then compares differential privacy, unlearning, regularization, and preference alignment for privacy-utility trade-offs.
- [Reconstructing Training Data from Adapter-based Federated Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.17533): Introduces UTR, a gradient-inversion attack that exploits frozen-layer attention and low-rank adapter gradients to reconstruct text from adapter-based federated LLM training.
- [AttenMIA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.18110): Uses attention signals for LLM membership-inference attacks, adding another model-internal privacy leakage route.
- [Ad Insertion in LLM-Generated Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19435): Studies sponsored-content insertion in LLM answers as a manipulation and disclosure risk, focusing on how generated responses can blend advertisements with task-oriented content.
- [GAVEL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.19768): Monitors activations to support rule-based safety control, adding a model-internal safety signal.
- [Reinforcement Unlearning via Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20568): Applies group relative policy optimization to reinforcement unlearning, connecting RL-style post-training with targeted forgetting.
- [ICON: Intent-Context Coupling for Efficient Multi-Turn Jailbreak Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20903): Couples malicious intent with benign conversational context across turns, targeting multi-turn jailbreak defenses that only screen isolated user messages.
- [Lossless Copyright Protection via Intrinsic Model Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21252): Embeds intrinsic model fingerprints for copyright protection so generated outputs can be attributed without degrading model utility.
- [Small models, big threats: Characterizing safety challenges from low-compute AI models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21365): Parent review candidate: characterizes misuse risks from low-compute models as a threat-landscape study, useful for safety prioritization but not clearly a model-side method.
- [Industrialized Deception: The Collateral Effects of LLM-Generated Misinformation on Digital Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.21963): Parent review candidate: contributes JudgeGPT and RogueGPT for studying human perception of AI-generated news, making it closer to misinformation evaluation infrastructure than a model method.
- [Visual-Guided Key-Token Regularization for Multimodal Large Language Model Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22020): Targets multimodal LLM unlearning through visual-guided key-token regularization, extending forgetting methods to MLLMs.
- [From Logits to Latents: Contrastive Representation Shaping for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22028): Adds CLReg, a contrastive representation regularizer that separates forget features from retain features to reduce latent entanglement left by prediction-space unlearning.
- [Per-parameter Task Arithmetic for Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22030): Uses per-parameter task arithmetic for LLM unlearning, adding a parameter-space forgetting mechanism.
- [The Unseen Threat: Residual Knowledge in Machine Unlearning under Perturbed Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22359): Defines residual knowledge as an unlearned model's ability to recognize perturbed forget samples and proposes RURK fine-tuning to penalize that local-neighborhood leakage.
- [Rethinking Anonymity Claims in Synthetic Data Generation: A Model-Centric Privacy Attack Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22434): Reframes synthetic-data anonymity around queryable generative models, mapping GDPR identifiability risks to privacy attacks and contrasting differential privacy with similarity metrics.
- [Private Code Autocomplete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22935): applies differential privacy to IDE autocomplete, linking code-assistant training with private-code leakage control.
- [Character as a Latent Variable in Large Language Models: A Mechanistic Account of Emergent Misalignment and Conditional Safety Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23081): Models character as a latent variable behind emergent misalignment.
- [THINKSAFE: Self-Generated Safety Alignment for Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.23143): Generates safety-alignment data for reasoning models to reduce harmful over-compliance after RL.
- [Shedding the Facades, Connecting the Domains: Detecting Shifting Multimodal Hate Video with Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00132): Introduces SCANNER, a hateful-video test-time adaptation method using centroid-guided stable-core alignment, adaptive centroid weighting, and intra-cluster diversity regularization.
- [Attention-Guided Feature Learning for Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00333): Learns steering features with attention guidance, reducing brittle concept extraction in activation-based LLM control.
- [SAU: Sparsity-Aware Unlearning for LLMs via Gradient Masking and Importance Redistribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00577): Uses sparsity-aware gradient masking and importance redistribution for LLM unlearning.
- [Small-Margin Preferences Still Matter-If You Train Them Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.00954): Proposes MixDPO, which orders preference pairs by margin difficulty and routes ambiguous pairs to SFT while applying preference loss to easier pairs.
- [Universal Transferable Jailbreak Attacks on VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01025): Studies universal and transferable jailbreak attacks on vision-language models.
- [Minimizing Mismatch Risk: A Prototype-Based Routing Framework for Zero-shot LLM-generated Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01240): Builds DetectRouter, a two-stage prototype router that selects the best surrogate detector for each unknown text source using white-box prototypes and black-box detection-score geometry.
- [Improving the Trade-off Between Watermark Strength and Speculative Sampling Efficiency for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01428): Characterizes watermark strength versus speculative-sampling acceptance as a constrained optimization problem and injects pseudorandomness into draft-token acceptance to preserve both detectability and speed.
- [Steering Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01654): Replaces static steering vectors with context-aware vector fields for more reliable inference-time LLM control.
- [Efficient Adversarial Attacks on High-dimensional Offline Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01658): Perturbs public reward models used by offline bandit evaluators, showing high-dimensional generative-model assessment can be hijacked with small targeted weight changes.
- [AGTAO: Robust and Stabilized LLM Unlearning via Adversarial Gating Training with Adaptive Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01703): Uses adversarial gating and adaptive orthogonality for robust LLM unlearning.
- [RedVisor: Reasoning-Aware Prompt Injection Defense via Zero-Copy KV Cache Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01795): Adds a removable adapter that localizes prompt-injection threats in a reasoning phase, conditions refusal, and reuses KV cache in vLLM to avoid the latency of separate detection pipelines.
- [Prediction-Powered Risk Monitoring of Deployed Models for Detecting Harmful Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02229): Proposes PPRM, which combines synthetic and scarce true labels into anytime-valid lower bounds on running risk for finite-sample false-alarm guarantees under harmful shifts.
- [CATNIP: LLM Unlearning via Calibrated and Tokenized Negative Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02824): CATNIP adds a safety model for jailbreaks, privacy, guardrails, unlearning, or adversarial robustness.
- [What Structural Inductive Bias Helps Transformers Reason Over Knowledge Graphs? A Study with Tabula RASA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02834): Parent review candidate: isolates sparse adjacency masking as the dominant transformer bias for multi-hop KGQA, a reasoning-architecture result with no direct agent-safety axis.
- [FaceLinkGen: Rethinking Identity Leakage in Privacy-Preserving Face Recognition with Identity Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02914): Attacks privacy-preserving face recognition templates with linkage and regeneration, exceeding 98.5% matching accuracy and showing PSNR/SSIM reconstruction metrics miss identity leakage.
- [Invisible Clean-Label Backdoor Attacks for Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03316): Introduces InvLBA, an invisible clean-label backdoor that perturbs latent features in generated augmentation images, improving attack success by 46.43% while preserving clean accuracy.
- [GuardReasoner-Omni: A Reasoning-based Multi-modal Guardrail for Text, Image, and Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03328): Trains 3B and 7B multimodal guardrail models on 181k text, image, video, and audio samples using reasoning SFT followed by RL with a correctness reward.
- [The Personality Trap: How LLMs Embed Bias When Generating Human-Like Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03334): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
