- [Entropy-driven sabotage: Informative unlearning attacks on diffusion model](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.neucom.2026.132644): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
- [Token-Anchored Semantic Drift: Untargeted Transferable Attacks Against Commercial MLLMs](https://doi.org/10.1109/gaiis69281.2026.11519348): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for transferable attacks against multimodal large language models.
- [Adversarial Diffusion Model: Generating High-Quality and Undetectable Images From Scratch](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2026.3657841): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
- [Selective Persuasion: Structural Stance Bias of Generative AI in Controversial Issues](https://doi.org/10.26599/gjms.2026.9330009): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
- [Efficient and Robust Bird’s-Eye-View Perception via State Space Models with Linear Complexity](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.dsp.2026.106166): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, reward, over-refusal, or monitoring method for language or multimodal models.
- [Improving face forgery detection via hierarchical mixture of experts and fine-grained visual-text alignment](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2026.113939): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
- [Large Language Models for Nodal Analysis in Circuits Education: An Evaluation](https://doi.org/10.1109/southeastcon63549.2026.11476570): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, reward, over-refusal, or monitoring method for language or multimodal models.
- [Towards Comprehensive Semi-Formal Verification of AMBA AHB/APB Interconnects: Configuration Coverage, Mutation Testing, and Safety-Aware Design-for-Verification](https://doi.org/10.1109/cipher70417.2026.11523956): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, reward, over-refusal, or monitoring method for language or multimodal models.
- [GS-mark: Deep Robust Watermarking for Graph Signals](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463492): Adds a model-side method, training recipe, architecture, synthetic-data method, reward/verifier, or world-model item for safety, privacy, robustness, watermarking, or adversarial evaluation contribution.
- [TFPA: Enhancing adversarial attack on speech recognition via Time–Frequency Pre-alignment](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2026.115992): Analyzes an adversarial risk through TFPA, focusing on the attack surface, transfer path, or defense failure mode.
- [Privacy Risks Associated with the Use of LLMs in Software Development](https://doi.org/10.5753/sbsi.2026.248323): Analyzes privacy leakage or protection through the Use of LLMs in Software Development, focusing on membership, data extraction, or sensitive-information exposure.
- [Ordinal-Aware Fine-Tuning of Multilingual LLaMA Models for Thai Cyberbullying Severity Classification](https://doi.org/10.1109/aaiml67890.2026.11498136): Measures or mitigates fairness and bias risk through Ordinal-Aware Fine-Tuning of Multilingual LLaMA Models for Thai Cyberbullying Severity Classification, treating demographic, severity, or moderation skew as the safety axis.
- [Defense-to-attack: Bypassing weak defenses enables stronger jailbreaks in Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2026.113805): Introduces or analyzes a jailbreak or prompt-injection attack using Bypassing weak defenses enables stronger jailbreaks in Vision-Language Models, focusing on how the prompt bypasses safety filters.
- [Comparative Analysis of LLMs Against Jailbreak Prompts](https://doi.org/10.1109/icecte69292.2026.11429310): Measures jailbreak or prompt-injection risk with Comparative Analysis of LLMs Against Jailbreak Prompts, separating unsafe-response behavior from ordinary task failure.
- [SALB: Security-Aware Load Balancing for Large Language Model Training in Datacenter Networks](https://doi.org/10.1109/tnsm.2026.3678979): Adds security-aware load balancing to LLM training datacenter networks, treating training infrastructure as the safety and privacy surface.
- [A Fine-Tuning Data Recovery Attack on Generative Language Models via Backdooring](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2026.3671126): Introduces or studies a backdoor or poisoning attack through Backdooring, focusing on trigger persistence, transfer, or data-recovery leakage.
- [ETV-Attack: Efficient text-driven visual-variable adversarial attacks on visual question answering with pre-trained language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.patcog.2026.113202): Analyzes an adversarial risk through ETV-Attack, focusing on the attack surface, transfer path, or defense failure mode.
- [Two-Layer Input Filtering Framework for Large Language Model Security](https://doi.org/10.1109/i3ctcon68242.2026.11507202): Uses a two-layer input-filtering framework for LLM security, separating risky prompts before they reach generation.
- [ROAD-tv: Research Opportunity Discovery via Topological Data Analysis and Adversarial Multi-LLM Validation](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.procs.2026.01.036): Analyzes an adversarial risk through ROAD-tv, focusing on the attack surface, transfer path, or defense failure mode.
- [A unified optimization framework for backdoor attacks in large language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.inffus.2026.104221): Adds a model-side contribution for agent safety: unified framework for backdoor attacks in LLMs.
- [Mid-Generation Jailbreaks in Open-Source LLMs Using a Pause-and-Edit Attack](https://doi.org/10.1109/icaic67076.2026.11395884): Adds a model-side contribution for agent safety: mid-generation jailbreak attack on open-source LLMs.
- [Toward Constitutional Autonomy in AI Systems: A Theoretical Framework for Aligned Agentic Intelligence](https://doi.org/10.1109/access.2026.3654907): Introduces a model-side method, training signal, architecture, or adaptation strategy for Agent Safety.
- [Modelling implicit bias in gender–career associations: A systematic comparison of language models](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ipm.2026.104840): Compares language-model encodings of gender-career associations, using implicit-bias measurement rather than a generic safety benchmark.
- [HybridCrypt-LLM: Lightweight privacy for LLM training and inference](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.eswa.2026.131632): Combines lightweight cryptographic protection with LLM training and inference so prompts, gradients, or intermediate data can be processed with reduced leakage.
- [A Unified Four-Stage Dynamic Cycle for Robust Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463011): Organizes robust federated LLM fine-tuning into a four-stage cycle for privacy-sensitive distributed adaptation rather than centralized data collection.
- [Selective Layer-Wise Cleansing: A Knowledge-Preserving Defense Against Backdoor Attacks in LoRA-Tuned Models for Natural Language Understanding](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.knosys.2026.115848): Removes LoRA backdoor behavior by cleansing selected layers while preserving task knowledge in natural-language-understanding adapters.
- [Federated Instruction-Tuning of Large Language Models with Privacy and Communication Efficiency](https://doi.org/10.1109/ccic68129.2026.11486066): Applies federated instruction tuning to LLMs with communication-efficient updates so private instruction data stays local.
- [Can Unlearning of Models Lead to Adversarial Robustness?](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11463115): Studies whether model unlearning changes adversarial robustness, making removal-oriented training relevant to safety and robustness tradeoffs.
- [Learning Multilingual Agentic Policy to Control Sycophancy](https://doi.org/10.18653/v1/2026.eacl-long.169): Learns a multilingual agentic policy to control sycophancy, fitting model-side alignment and agent-safety mitigation.
- [Defending LLMs against jailbreak attacks through representation offset detection](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ipm.2026.104662): Defends LLMs against jailbreak attacks through representation offset detection.
- [Beyond the prompt: Log-based threat detection and attribution for multi-Agent LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.ipm.2026.104768): Uses logs for threat detection and attribution in multi-agent LLM systems.
- [Quantifying Risk Propagation in Agentic AI Networks: A Simulation Framework and Singularity Index Approach](https://doi.org/10.1109/icaiii69475.2026.11521777): Quantifies risk propagation in agentic AI networks with a simulation framework.
- [Enhancing Risk Awareness in LLM Agents via Probing Safety Boundaries](https://doi.org/10.1109/icassp55912.2026.11464257): Improves LLM-agent risk awareness by probing safety boundaries.
- [Risk-Aware Privacy Preservation for LLM Inference](https://doi.org/10.1109/tifs.2026.3667458): A model, training, or algorithmic contribution for agent safety and security evaluation. Core idea: focus on risk-aware privacy preservation for llm inference as a capability-relevant model reference.
- [RoLLMRec: a robust LLM-based recommender system for defending against shilling and prompt injection attacks](https://doi.org/10.3389/fcomp.2026.1735253): Defends an LLM-based recommender against shilling and prompt-injection attacks.
- [Adversarial robustness of LLM-based multi-agent systems for engineering problems](https://doi.org/10.3389/frai.2026.1784484): Studies adversarial robustness of LLM-based multi-agent systems for engineering problems.
- [Adaptive Adversarial Training for Balancing Model Robustness and Standard Performance](https://doi.org/10.3724/2096-7004.di.2025.0182): Tunes adversarial training to balance robustness against standard-task performance.
- [Safety Misalignment Against LLMs](https://www.ndss-symposium.org/ndss-paper/safety-misalignment-against-large-language-models/): Studies safety-misalignment attacks and defenses for LLMs through representation-level alignment behavior.
- [Causally Motivated Sycophancy Mitigation](https://openreview.net/forum?id=yRKelogz5i): Targets sycophancy mitigation through causal modeling, adding a model-side alignment method for reducing agreement-seeking behavior.
- [Semantic-Graph Defense](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v40i5.37389): Defends LLMs against adversarial prompts by disentangling prompt semantics with graph-based structure.
- [BadCodePrompt](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10515-024-00485-2): Studies backdoor attacks against prompt-engineered LLM code generation, adding a code-oriented model-safety threat.
- [On Effects of Steering Latent Representation for Large Language Model Unlearning](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i22.34544): Studies latent-representation steering for LLM unlearning, clarifying model-side forgetting and retention trade-offs.
- [Towards Safe Machine Unlearning: A Paradigm that Mitigates Performance Degradation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3696410.3714638): Frames safe machine unlearning as a way to reduce performance degradation, adding a model-side safety and forgetting reference.
- [TransLock: Securing LLM deployment for software applications via self-locking watermarks](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10664-025-10750-x): Secures LLM deployment for software applications through self-locking watermarks, adding a model-provenance and deployment-integrity safety mechanism.
- [Scalable watermarking for identifying large language model outputs](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-024-08025-4): Introduces scalable watermarking to identify language-model outputs.
- [MoGU: A Framework for Enhancing Safety of LLMs While Preserving Their Usability](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-2780): Tunes safety behavior with a usability-preserving guard framework, targeting refusal control without collapsing normal helpfulness.
- [RAG-leaks: difficulty-calibrated membership inference attacks on retrieval-augmented generation](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11432-024-4441-4): Calibrates membership-inference attacks for RAG systems by difficulty, exposing when retrieved documents can be inferred from model responses.
- [KGDist: A Prompt-Based Distillation Attack against LMs Augmented with Knowledge Graphs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3678890.3678906): Uses crafted prompts to distill knowledge from language models augmented with knowledge graphs, treating the KG-enhanced model as an extraction target.
- [Enhancing Character-Coherent Role-Playing Dialogue with a Verifiable Emotion Reward](https://doi.org/10.3390/info16090738): Introduces a frozen-classifier Verifiable Emotion Reward and CHARCO dialogues to reduce emotional drift in long role-playing conversations.
- [Artificial Intelligence Risk Management Framework: Generative Artificial Intelligence Profile](https://www.nist.gov/publications/artificial-intelligence-risk-management-framework-generative-artificial-intelligence): Adapts the NIST AI RMF into a generative-AI risk profile covering content provenance, data leakage, misuse, and monitoring controls.
- [The life cycle of large language models in education: A framework for understanding sources of bias](https://doi.org/10.1111/bjet.13505): Maps bias sources across educational LLM deployment, from training data and model behavior to classroom use and evaluation feedback.
- [STELA: a community-centred approach to norm elicitation for AI alignment](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-024-56648-4): Provides a participatory norm-elicitation process for choosing whose values should guide AI alignment rather than hard-coding a single normative source.
- [Backdoor Attacks via Machine Unlearning](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v38i13.29321): Shows machine unlearning itself can implant backdoors, turning forget requests into a security attack surface.
- [Guardian: A Runtime Framework for LLM-Based UI Exploration](https://doi.org/10.1145/3650212.3680334): Wraps LLM-based UI exploration in runtime checks that block unsafe GUI actions during automated application testing.
- [Imperceptible Content Poisoning in LLM-Powered Applications](https://doi.org/10.1145/3691620.3695001): Introduces or studies a backdoor or poisoning attack through Imperceptible Content Poisoning in LLM-Powered Applications, focusing on trigger persistence, transfer, or data-recovery leakage.
- [The Earth alignment principle for artificial intelligence](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41893-025-01536-6): Frames AI alignment around planetary sustainability constraints, making environmental impact a safety objective rather than only a deployment externality.
- [SecureVision: Advanced Cybersecurity Deepfake Detection with Big Data Analytics](https://doi.org/10.3390/s24196300): Combines multimodal audio-visual deepfake detection with big-data analytics to flag manipulated media for cybersecurity use cases.
- [Governing Open Vocabulary Data Leaks Using an Edge LLM through Programming by Example](https://doi.org/10.1145/3699760): Implements GPTWall, an edge-LLM privacy firewall whose programming-by-example policies detect open-vocabulary prompt data leaks before external LLM calls.
- [ToxVI: a Multimodal LLM-based Framework for Generating Intervention in Toxic Code-Mixed Videos](https://doi.org/10.1145/3627673.3680004): Uses a multimodal LLM pipeline to detect toxicity in code-mixed videos and generate intervention responses tied to the visual and textual context.
- [A bias towards neutrality? How LLM guardrail sensitivity affects classification](https://doi.org/10.1007/s44382-025-00013-0): Shows LLM safety guardrails can skew automated social-science labeling toward neutral classes, creating a measurement bias in classifier outputs.
- [A physics‐informed train on synthetic and test on real method for evaluating large language model‐generated safety‐critical traffic scenarios](https://doi.org/10.1111/mice.70071): Uses physics-informed synthetic-to-real testing to judge whether LLM-generated traffic scenarios preserve safety-critical dynamics.
- [Real-Time 3D Scene Understanding for Road Safety: Depth Estimation and Object Detection for Autonomous Vehicle Awareness](https://doi.org/10.3390/vehicles8020028): Builds a road-safety perception pipeline with FoundationStereo depth estimation and object detection running in real time on embedded hardware.
- [Robust Multilingual Audio Deepfake Detection Through Hybrid Modeling](https://doi.org/10.1145/3733102.3736706): Combines acoustic and multilingual modeling signals to detect audio deepfakes across languages rather than a single-speaker or English-only setting.
- [The gains do not make up for the losses: a comprehensive evaluation for safety alignment of large language models via machine unlearning](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11704-024-41099-x): Evaluates LLM safety unlearning beyond harmful-content removal, showing utility and knowledge losses can outweigh alignment gains.
- [RogueGPT: Unleashing Jailbreak Prompts on LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1002/eng2.70069): Studies instruction-tuning-style jailbreak prompts that push LLMs toward policy-violating responses and compares exploitation routes.
- [Unveiling the Risk of Unsafe Image Generation in Stable Diffusion Through a Cross-Attention Mechanism](https://doi.org/10.3390/fi18050248): Introduces EvilPrompt, a text-only Stable Diffusion jailbreak that exploits cross-attention behavior to elicit unsafe image generation.
- [Privacy Matters: Data Attack to Make User Preferences Unlearnable in Recommendation](https://doi.org/10.1145/3803545): Designs a user-side data attack that makes recommender systems fail to learn private preferences while preserving ordinary recommendation interactions.
- [Jailbreak attack of large language model based on scene construction](https://doi.org/10.1145/3708036.3708083): Introduces or analyzes a jailbreak or prompt-injection attack using scene construction, focusing on how the prompt bypasses safety filters.
- [Mitigating adversarial manipulation in LLMs: a prompt-based approach to counter Jailbreak attacks (Prompt-G)](https://doi.org/10.7717/peerj-cs.2374): Uses Prompt-G prompt rewriting and classification cues to counter jailbreak manipulation while tracking the safety-accuracy trade-off.
- [LLM-Fuzzer: Scaling Assessment of Large Language Model Jailbreaks](https://www.usenix.org/conference/usenixsecurity24/presentation/yu-jiahao): Scales jailbreak discovery with a fuzzing loop that mutates prompts and measures harmful-response success across target LLMs.
- [Using Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) for Automated Detection of Traffic Safety-Critical Events](https://doi.org/10.3390/vehicles6030074): Prompts MLLMs with object-level questions to detect traffic safety-critical events from multimodal driving data.
- [Pandora's Box: Towards Building Universal Attackers against Real-World Large Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.52202/079017-1652): Builds universal VLM attackers that transfer across real-world vision-language services rather than optimizing a single local model.
- [Adaptive bidirectional planning framework for enhanced safety and robust decision-making in autonomous navigation systems](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11227-025-07389-2): Uses adaptive bidirectional planning to improve autonomous-navigation safety and robust decisions under uncertain routes.
- [A Reliable Generative Adversarial Network Approach for Climate Downscaling and Weather Generation](https://doi.org/10.1029/2024ms004668): Uses GAN-based stochastic downscaling to model high-impact weather distributions, an adjacent risk-modeling method rather than an LLM guard.
- [Text-Based Prompt Injection Attack Using Mathematical Functions in Modern Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics13245008): Encodes prompt-injection instructions as mathematical functions to bypass modern LLM safety policies with text-only inputs.
- [Advancing Generalized Deepfake Detector with Forgery Perception Guidance](https://doi.org/10.1145/3664647.3680713): Guides deepfake detectors with forgery-perception signals so they generalize across manipulation types and generation sources.
- [Multiscale Features Integrated Model for Generalizable Deepfake Detection](https://doi.org/10.1155/int/7084582): Fuses multiscale texture and semantic features to improve deepfake detection on images from unseen generative models.
- [Deepfake, Real Harm: A Participatory Approach for Imagining Infrastructures to Combat Deepfake Sexual Abuse](https://doi.org/10.1145/3772318.3790902): Uses participatory design to define response infrastructures for deepfake sexual abuse, making this a deferred governance and platform-safety item rather than a detector.
- [Evading LLMs’ Safety Boundary with Adaptive Role-Play Jailbreaking](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics14244808): Searches role settings that erode LLM refusal behavior and uses adaptive role-play prompts to elicit harmful answers.
- [Signed distance function–biased flow importance sampling for implicit neural compression of flow fields](https://doi.org/10.1111/mice.13526): Improves implicit neural compression of flow fields with signed-distance-function-biased sampling, an off-axis engineering model for later taxonomy review.
- [Discovering Safety Violations of Decision-Making in Autonomous Driving Systems from Accident-Free Traffic Scenarios](https://doi.org/10.1145/3787978): Generates critical autonomous-driving test scenarios from accident-free traffic to uncover decision-making safety violations.
- [Seeing is No Longer Believing: How Deepfakes May Shape the Future of Identity Credibility in Media](https://doi.org/10.1145/3733813.3764371): Analyzes how deepfakes weaken media identity credibility, a deferred socio-technical risk item rather than a model detector.
- [Chain-of-Thought Guided Semantic Debiasing for Low-Shot Vision-Language Tasks](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755512): Uses chain-of-thought-guided semantic debiasing to reduce low-shot vision-language bias without large task-specific datasets.
- [Whose weather is it? A fairness framework for data-driven weather forecasting](https://doi.org/10.1088/1748-9326/ae21f5): Defines statistical-parity and conditional-parity fairness criteria for data-driven weather forecasts across protected geographic groups.
- [Plane-wave decomposition and randomised training; a novel path to generalised physics-informed neural networks for simple harmonic motion](https://doi.org/10.1088/2632-2153/ae143b): Trains PINNs on randomized boundary conditions and plane-wave decompositions, an off-axis physics model that should be revisited outside Agent Safety.
- [Stack More LLM’s: Efficient Detection of Machine-Generated Texts via Perplexity Approximation](https://doi.org/10.1134/s1064562424602075): Detects machine-generated text by stacking LLM perplexity approximations into a lightweight discriminator.
- [Research on adversarial attack and defense of large language models](https://doi.org/10.54254/2755-2721/93/20240922): Reviews LLM adversarial attack and defense mechanisms, especially defense design choices against prompt-level attacks.
- [Revealing the Difficulty in Jailbreak Defense on Language Models for Metaverse](https://doi.org/10.1145/3698387.3699998): Tests jailbreak defenses in metaverse-oriented language-model settings, exposing where virtual-world prompts defeat ordinary filters.
- [MnMR-GenA: a morphological recombination genetic algorithm for jailbreak attacks in low-resource language](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-47434-5): Uses morphological recombination and a genetic search loop to craft jailbreak prompts in low-resource languages.
- [Feature-indistinguishable machine unlearning via negative-hot label encoding and class weight masking](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-40379-9): Performs class-level unlearning with negative-hot labels and class weight masking while making forgotten features hard to distinguish.
- [UniAttack: Unified Physical-Digital Face Attack Detection](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11263-025-02673-3): Unifies physical and digital face-attack detection so the same detector handles presentation attacks and manipulated face imagery.
- [Fast and Accurate Class-Level Machine Unlearning using Impair–Repair and Noise-Induced Forgetting Mechanisms](https://doi.org/10.1007/s12559-026-10597-4): Combines impair-repair updates with noise-induced forgetting for fast class-level unlearning while recovering retained-class accuracy.
- [How do large-language models respond to moral dilemmas? Insights from the defining issues test](https://doi.org/10.1007/s43681-026-01104-4): Uses the Defining Issues Test to compare LLM responses to moral dilemmas, surfacing alignment behavior rather than proposing a guard model.
- [Spatial‐Frequency Feature Fusion Based Deepfake Detection With Mask Supervision](https://doi.org/10.1111/exsy.70218): Adds mask supervision to spatial-frequency feature fusion so deepfake detectors focus on manipulated facial regions.
- [A MoE-based Safety Fine-tuning Method for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3725899.3725911): Applies mixture-of-experts safety fine-tuning to MLLMs so safety-related experts can specialize without degrading multimodal utility.
- [Pseudo-Inverse Prefix Tuning for Effective Unlearning in LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746252.3760939): Uses pseudo-inverse prefix tuning as a parameter-efficient route for removing targeted LLM knowledge.
- [Rethinking the Training Paradigm of Discrete Token-Based Multimodal LLMs: An Analysis of Text-Centric Bias](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746252.3761264): Diagnoses text-centric bias in discrete-token multimodal LLM training and points to training changes that rebalance visual and textual signals.
- [LLM-Mod: Can Large Language Models Assist Content Moderation?](https://doi.org/10.1145/3613905.3650828): Evaluates LLMs as moderation assistants, testing how well generated judgments support content-policy decisions.
- [Examining Human Perception of Generative Content Replacement in Image Privacy Protection](https://doi.org/10.1145/3613904.3642103): Studies whether replacing sensitive image regions with generated content preserves privacy while remaining acceptable to human viewers.
- [CapAlign: Improving Cross Modal Alignment via Informative Captioning for Harmful Meme Detection](https://doi.org/10.1145/3589334.3648146): Improves harmful-meme detection by generating informative captions that align visual entities with hateful or harmful text cues.
- [Predictive Risk-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Vehicles Using Safety Potential](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics14224446): Applies Safety Potential to safety-critical robot, vehicle, or traffic decisions, focusing on violation prediction and risk-aware control.
- [Breaking through safety performance stagnation in autonomous vehicles with dense learning](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-026-69761-x): Applies safety performance stagnation in autonomous vehicles with dense learning to safety-critical robot, vehicle, or traffic decisions, focusing on violation prediction and risk-aware control.
- [When Hallucinated Concepts Cross Modals: Unveiling Backdoor Vulnerability in Multi-modal In-context Learning](https://doi.org/10.1145/3743093.3770938): Introduces or studies a backdoor or poisoning attack through Unveiling Backdoor Vulnerability in Multi-modal In-context Learning, focusing on trigger persistence, transfer, or data-recovery leakage.
- [Automatic Generation of Job Safety Reports with Explainable RAG-Based LLMs](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10796-025-10634-x): Generates job safety reports with explainable RAG-based LLMs, grounding safety documentation in retrieved evidence and explanations.
- [From Vibe Coding to Jailbreaking in Large Language Models: A Comparative Security Study](https://doi.org/10.3390/engproc2026123008): Introduces or analyzes a jailbreak or prompt-injection attack using A Comparative Security Study, focusing on how the prompt bypasses safety filters.
- [Exploring and Exploiting Security Vulnerabilities in Self-Hosted LLM Services](https://doi.org/10.1145/3774904.3792097): Analyzes an adversarial risk through Exploring and Exploiting Security Vulnerabilities in Self-Hosted LLM Services, focusing on the attack surface, transfer path, or defense failure mode.
- [Nexus scissor: enhance open-access language model safety by connection pruning](https://doi.org/10.1038/s44387-025-00046-2): Improves open-access language-model safety by pruning unsafe model connections, treating connection removal as a lightweight safety intervention.
- [SEDM: A Safety‐Enhanced Decision‐Making Framework for Autonomous Driving by Integrating Large Language Models and XGBoost](https://doi.org/10.1049/itr2.70178): Applies SEDM to safety-critical robot, vehicle, or traffic decisions, focusing on violation prediction and risk-aware control.
- [Detection and analysis of prompt injection in indian multilingual large language models](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41598-026-43883-0): Detects and analyzes prompt-injection behavior in Indian multilingual LLMs, using multilingual prompts as the attack surface.
- [Securing LLM-in-the-loop software for empirical study of risks, mitigations, and utility trade-offs in a safety-critical case](https://doi.org/10.1007/s10664-026-10820-8): Empirically studies risks, mitigations, and utility trade-offs for LLM-in-the-loop software in a safety-critical deployment case.
- [TF-IDF joint SVM model in library automation bias risk assessment system](https://doi.org/10.1007/s44163-026-00978-x): Uses a TF-IDF and SVM pipeline to assess bias risk in library automation, treating classifier severity output as the risk signal.
- [Visual–Language Model–Driven Annotation and Analysis of Maneuver Data for Enhanced Vehicle Safety](https://doi.org/10.4271/09-14-01-0032): Uses visual-language models to annotate and analyze vehicle maneuver data, targeting safety-relevant driving behavior signals.
- [SM-GCG: Spatial Momentum Greedy Coordinate Gradient for Robust Jailbreak Attacks on Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/electronics14193967): Introduces or analyzes a jailbreak or prompt-injection attack using SM-GCG, focusing on how the prompt bypasses safety filters.
- [Evaluating and Mitigating Sycophancy in Large Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746027.3755778): Adds model-side work for agent and model safety, alignment, privacy, bias, guardrail, or misuse-resistance capability, including training, adaptation, data, reward, verifier, inference-scaling, or test-time methods.

- [PromptTrace: A Fine-Grained Prompt Stealing Attack via CLIP-Guided Beam Search for Text-to-Image Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/sym18010161): Reconstructs text-to-image prompts from generated images by decomposing prompt attributes and using CLIP-guided beam search under black-box access.

- [A Hybrid Perplexity-MAS Framework for Proactive Jailbreak Attack Detection in Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.3390/app152413190): Adds model-side work for agent safety, risk control, prompt injection, privacy, and harmful-content evaluation, such as training, architecture, alignment, memory, reward, verification, world modeling, or test-time adaptation.
- [See, Localize and Verify: A GRPO-Powered Framework for Enhancing Factual Accuracy in Multimodal Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746276.3760467): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [AllGuard: A Multimodal Large Language Model for Edge-Deployed Content Security Assessment](https://doi.org/10.1007/s11390-025-5508-7): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [Video forgery detection based on multilocal feature and spatiotemporal fusion](https://doi.org/10.1117/1.jei.34.4.043002): Adds a model-side method, training or post-training recipe, architecture, data-generation method, verifier, world model, or inference-time technique for agent and LLM safety.
- [A Differential Privacy-Based Mechanism for Preventing Data Leakage in Large Language Model Training](https://doi.org/10.70393/616a736d.323732): Adds a privacy-preserving training mechanism for preventing data leakage in large language models.
- [Reinforcement Learning-Guided Large Language Model Fine-Tuning for Privacy-Preserving Text Rewriting](https://doi.org/10.1145/3769102.3774433): Adds model-side coverage for agent and model safety, focusing on privacy-preserving text rewriting through RL-guided LLM fine-tuning.
- [Common Learning Constraints Alter Interpretations of Direct Preference Optimization](https://proceedings.mlr.press/v258/kong25a.html): Adds model-side coverage for agent and model safety, focusing on DPO interpretation under common learning constraints.
- [Deepfake Image Detection: A Universal Approach via Vision-Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1145/3784833.3784856): Builds FEFA, a VLM-based detector with a forgery-aware adapter in the image encoder plus frequency-band attention and detail enhancement for cross-dataset deepfake-image generalization.
- [Technique for Identifying Texts Generated by Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.15622/ia.24.5.7): Proposes a technique for identifying text generated by large language models.
- [Beyond Text: Leveraging Vision-Language Models for Misinformation Detection](https://doi.org/10.1145/3746275.3762205): Uses vision-language models for misinformation detection beyond text-only signals.
- [Adversarial Defense without Adversarial Defense: Enhancing Language Model Robustness via Instance-level Principal Component Removal](https://doi.org/10.1162/tacl.a.43): Adds an instance-level robustness method for language models.

- [PoisonedEye: Knowledge Poisoning Attack on Retrieval-Augmented Generation based Large Vision-Language Models](https://www.semanticscholar.org/paper/3ad12c0c4fce3b8b2f58f2d27eae365ad0132415): Studies knowledge poisoning attacks on retrieval-augmented large vision-language models.
- [CertPHash](https://www.usenix.org/conference/usenixsecurity25/presentation/yang-yuchen): Adds certified perceptual hashing for illicit-content screening through robust training with anti-evasion, anti-collision, and functionality objectives, giving image-hash safety systems provable robustness targets.
